<tr><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>1</td><td>Bispyribac<br>CAS No<br>125401-75-4<br>CIPAC No<br>748</td><td>2,6-bis(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-yloxy)benzoic acid</td><td>≥ 930 g/kg (referred to as bispyribac-sodium)</td><td>1 August 2011</td><td>31 July 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide in rice may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on bispyribac, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures where appropriate.<br>The Member States concerned shall request the submission of further information as regards the possible groundwater contamination by metabolites M03, M04 and M10.<br>They shall ensure that the applicant provides such information to the Commission by 31 July 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td>Profoxydim<br>CAS No 139001-49-3<br>CIPAC No 621</td><td>2 - [(1 <em>E/Z</em> ) - [(2 <em>R S</em> ) – 2 - (4 – chlorophenoxy) propoxyimino] butyl] – 3 – hydroxy – 5 - [(3 <em>R S</em> ; 3 <em>S R</em> ) – tetrahydro – 2 H – thiopyran – 3 – yl] cyclohex – 2 - enone</td><td>≥ 940 g/kg</td><td>1 August 2011</td><td>31 July 2021</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide in rice may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on profoxydim, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions,<br>the long-term risk to non-target organisms.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>3</td><td>Azimsulfuron<br>CAS No 120162-55-2<br>CIPAC No 584</td><td>1-(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-yl)-3-[1-methyl-4-(2-methyl-2H-tetrazol-5-yl)-pyrazol-5-ylsulfonyl]-urea</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg<br>maximum level of the impurity phenol 2 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2021</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide may be authorised.<br>Aerial applications may not be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on azimsulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((1)) the protection of non-target plants;<br>((2)) the potential for groundwater contamination, when the active substance is applied in vulnerable scenarios and/or climatic conditions;<br>((3)) the protection of aquatic organisms.<br>Member States shall ensure that the conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate (e.g. buffer zones, in rice cultivation minimum holding periods for water prior to discharge).<br>The notifier shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the risk assessment on aquatic organisms;<br>((b)) the identification of the degradation products in the aqueous photolysis of the substance.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Member States, the Commission and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>4</td><td>Azoxystrobin<br>CAS No 131860-33-8<br>CIPAC No 571</td><td>methyl (E)-2-{2[6-(2-cyanophenoxy)pyrimidin-4-yloxy]phenyl}-3-methoxyacrylate</td><td>≥ 930 g/kg<br>Toluene maximum content 2 g/kg<br>Z-isomer maximum content 25 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on azoxystrobin and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((1)) the fact that the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured must be confirmed and supported by appropriate analytical data. The test material used in the toxicity dossiers should be compared and verified against this specification of the technical material;<br>((2)) the potential for groundwater contamination, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((3)) the protection of aquatic organisms.<br>The Member States must ensure that the conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The Member States concerned shall request the submission of confirmatory information as regards the risk assessment on groundwater and aquatic organisms.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Member States, the Commission and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>5</td><td>Imazalil<br>CAS No 35554-44-0<br>73790-28-0 (replaced)<br>CIPAC No 335</td><td>(RS)-1-(β-allyloxy-2,4-dichlorophenethyl)imidazole<br>or<br>allyl (RS)-1-(2,4-dichlorophenyl)-2-imidazol-1-ylethyl ether</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on imazalil, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall:<br>((1)) pay particular attention to the fact that the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured must be confirmed and supported by appropriate analytical data. The test material used in the toxicity dossiers should be compared and verified against this specification of the technical material;<br>((2)) pay particular attention to the acute dietary exposure situation of consumers in view of future revisions of maximum residue levels;<br>((3)) pay particular attention to the operators and workers safety. Authorised conditions of use must prescribe the application of adequate personal protective equipment and risk mitigation measures to reduce the exposure;<br>((4)) ensure that appropriate waste management practices to handle the waste solution remaining after application, such as the cleaning water of the drenching system and the discharge of the processing waste are put in place. Prevention of any accidental spillage of treatment solution. Member States permitting the release of waste water into the sewage system shall ensure that a local risk assessment is carried out;<br>((5)) pay particular attention to risk to aquatic organisms and soil micro-organisms and long-term risk to granivorous birds and mammals.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The notifier shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) route of degradation of imazalil in soil and surface water systems;<br>((b)) environmental data to support the managing measures that Member States have to put in place to ensure that groundwater exposure is negligible;<br>((c)) a hydrolysis study to investigate the nature of residues in processed commodities.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Member States, the Commission and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>6</td><td>Prohexadione<br>CAS No 127277-53-6 <em>(prohexadione-calcium)</em><br>CIPAC No 567 <em>(prohexadione)</em><br>No 567.020 <em>(prohexadione-calcium)</em></td><td>3,5-dioxo-4-propionylcyclohexanecarboxylic acid</td><td>≥ 890 g/kg<br>(expressed as prohexadione-calcium)</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2022</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as plant growth regulator may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on prohexadione and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.</td></tr>
<tr><td>7</td><td>Spiroxamine<br>CAS No 1181134-30-8<br>CIPAC No 572</td><td>8- <em>tert</em> -butyl-1,4-dioxaspiro[4.5]decan-2-ylmethyl(ethyl)(propyl)amine (ISO)</td><td>≥ 940 g/kg<br>(diastereomers A and B combined)</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on spiroxamine, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((1)) the risk to operators and workers and ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment;<br>((2)) the protection of the groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((3)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The notifier shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the possible impact on the worker, the consumer and the environmental risk assessment of the potential stereo-selective degradation of each isomer in plant, animals and the environment;<br>((b)) the toxicity of the plant metabolites formed in fruit crops and the potential hydrolysis of fruit crop residues in processed commodities;<br>((c)) the groundwater exposure assessment for metabolite M03;<br>((d)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Member States, the Commission and the Authority the information set out in point (a) by two years after the adoption of specific guidance and the information set out in points (b), (c) and (d) by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>8</td><td>Kresoxim-methyl<br>CAS No 143 390-89-0<br>CIPAC No 568</td><td>methyl (E)-methoxyimino[a-(o-tolyloxy)-o-tolyl]acetate</td><td>≥ 910 g/kg<br>Methanol: max. 5 g/kg<br>Methyl chloride: max. 1 g/kg<br>Toluene: max. 1 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on kresoxim-methyl and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011, shall be taken into account.<br>Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater under vulnerable conditions; the conditions of authorisation shall include, where appropriate, risk mitigation measures.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>Groundwater exposure risk assessment, and in particular:<br>on the lysimeter study to support the statement that the two unidentified peaks observed do not correspond to metabolites individually exceeding the trigger value of 0,1 μg/L,<br>on the recovery of metabolite BF 490-5 in order to confirm its absence in the lysimeter leachate at levels exceeding 0,1 μg/L,<br>on a groundwater exposure risk assessment for the late application in apples, pears and grapes.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Member States, the Commission and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>9</td><td>Fluroxypyr<br>CAS No 69377-81-7<br>CIPAC No 431</td><td>4-amino-3,5-dichloro-6-fluoro-2-pyridyloxyacetic acid</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg (fluroxypyr-meptyl)<br>The following manufacturing impurity is of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following amount in the technical material:<br>N-methyl-2-pyrrolidone (NMP): &lt; 3 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on fluroxypyr, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 23 March 2017 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States must pay particular attention to:<br>the potential contamination of groundwater by metabolite fluroxypyr pyridinol, when the active substance is applied in regions with alkaline or vulnerable soil or with vulnerable climatic conditions;<br>the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>10</td><td>Tefluthrin<br>CAS No: <em>79538-32-2</em><br>CIPAC No: 451</td><td>2,3,5,6-tetrafluoro-4-methylbenzyl (1 <em>RS, 3RS</em> )-3-[( <em>Z</em> )-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoroprop-1-enyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate<br>Tefluthrin is a 1:1 mixture of <em>Z</em> -(1 <em>R</em> , 3 <em>R</em> ) and <em>Z</em> -(1 <em>S</em> , 3 <em>S</em> ) enantiomers.</td><td>≥ 920 g/kg<br>Hexachlorobenzene: not more than 1 mg/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as insecticide may be authorised.<br>The seed coating shall only be performed in professional seed treatment facilities. These facilities shall apply the best available techniques in order to exclude the release of dust clouds during storage, transport and application.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on tefluthrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the operators and workers safety and include among the authorised conditions of use the application of adequate personal protective equipment as well as respiratory protective equipment,<br>the risk to birds and mammals. Risk mitigation measures should be applied to grant a high degree of incorporation in soil and avoidance of spillage,<br>ensure that the label of treated seed includes the indication that the seeds were treated with tefluthrin and sets out the risk mitigation measures provided for in the authorisation.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the specification of the technical material, as commercially manufactured;<br>((2)) a validated analytical method for water;<br>((3)) the possible environmental impact of the preferential degradation/conversion of the isomers and an estimation of the relative toxicity and risk assessment for the workers.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in point (1) by 30 June 2012, the information set out in point (2) by 31 December 2012, and the information set out in point (3) 2 years after the adoption of a specific guidance document on evaluation of isomers mixture.</td></tr>
<tr><td>11</td><td>Oxyfluorfen<br>CAS No 42874-03-3<br>CIPAC No 538</td><td>2-chloro-α,α,α-trifluoro- <em>p</em> -tolyl 3-ethoxy-4-nitrophenyl ether</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>N,N-dimethylnitrosamine: not more than 50 μg/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide for banded applications close to ground from autumn to early spring may be authorised, at a rate not exceeding 150 g active substance per hectare, per year.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on oxyfluorfen, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States must pay particular attention to:<br>operator safety and ensure that conditions of use impose the application of adequate personal protective equipment where appropriate,<br>the risks to aquatic organisms, earthworm-eating mammals, soil-living macro-organisms, non-target arthropods and non-target plants.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures such as no-spray buffer zones and drift reducing nozzles and shall provide for respective labelling of plant protection products. Those conditions shall include further risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>12</td><td>1-naphthylacetamide<br>CAS No 86-86-2<br>CIPAC No 282</td><td>2-(1-naphthyl)acetamide</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as plant growth regulator may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 1-naphthylacetamide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to operators and workers and ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms;<br>((d)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to non-target plants;<br>((e)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to birds.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the risk to non-target plants;<br>((2)) the long-term risk to birds.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>13</td><td>1-naphthylacetic acid<br>CAS No 86-87-3<br>CIPAC No 313</td><td>1-naphthylacetic acid</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as plant growth regulator may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 1-naphthylacetic acid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to operators and workers and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the dietary exposure situation of consumers in view of future revisions of maximum residue levels;<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((d)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms;<br>((e)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to birds.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the route and rate of degradation in soil including an assessment of the potential for photolysis;<br>((2)) the long-term risk to birds.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>14</td><td>Fluquinconazole<br>CAS No 136426-54-5<br>CIPAC No 474</td><td>3-(2,4-dichlorophenyl)-6-fluoro-2-(1 <em>H</em> -1,2,4-triazol-1-yl)quinazolin-4(3 <em>H</em> )-one</td><td>≥ 955 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2021</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on fluquinconazole, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to operators and workers and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the dietary exposure of consumers to the residues of triazole derivative metabolites (TDMs);<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to birds and mammals.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) residues of triazole derivative metabolites (TDMs) in primary crops, rotational crops and products of animal origin;<br>((2)) the contribution of the potential residues of the metabolite dione in rotational crops to the overall consumer exposure;<br>((3)) the acute risk to insectivorous mammals;<br>((4)) the long-term risk to insectivorous and herbivorous birds and mammals;<br>((5)) the risk to earthworm-eating mammals;<br>((6)) the endocrine disruption potential in aquatic organisms (fish full life cycle study).<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>15</td><td>Fluazifop P<br>CAS No 83066-88-0 (fluazifop-P)<br>CIPAC No 467 (fluazifop-P)</td><td>( <em>R</em> )-2-{4-[5-(trifluoromethyl)-2-pyridyloxy]phenoxy}propionic acid (fluazifop-P)</td><td>≥ 900 g/kg in fluazifop P-butyl<br>The following impurity 2-chloro-5-(trifluoromethyl)pyridine must not exceed 1,5 g/kg in the material as manufactured.</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on fluazifop-P, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 1 February 2013, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall:<br>pay particular attention to consumer safety as regards the occurrence in groundwater of the metabolite compound X;<br>pay particular attention to operator safety and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>pay particular attention to the protection of surface water and groundwater in vulnerable zones;<br>pay particular attention to the risk for non-target plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the specification of the technical material, as commercially manufactured, including information on the relevance of the impurity R154719;<br>((2)) the equivalence between the specifications of the technical material, as commercially manufactured, and the specifications of the test material used in the toxicity studies;<br>((3)) the potential long-term risk to herbivorous mammals;<br>((4)) the fate and behaviour in the environment of the metabolite compounds X and IV;<br>((5)) the potential risk to fish and aquatic invertebrates for the metabolite compound IV.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in points (1) and (2) by 30 June 2012 and the information set out in points (3), (4) and (5) by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>16</td><td>Terbuthylazine<br>CAS No 5915-41-3<br>CIPAC No 234</td><td>N2-tert-butyl-6-chloro-N4-ethyl-1,3,5-triazine-2,4-diamine</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Propazine not more than 10 g/kg<br>Atrazine not more than 1 g/kg<br>Simazine not more than 30 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as herbicide may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on terbuthylazine, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the protection of groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((b)) the risk to mammals and earthworms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures and the obligation to carry out monitoring programmes to verify potential groundwater contamination in vulnerable zones, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the specification of the technical material, as commercially manufactured, by appropriate analytical data, including information on the relevance of the impurities;<br>((2)) the equivalence between the specifications of the technical material, as commercially manufactured, and the specifications of the test material used in the toxicity studies;<br>((3)) groundwater exposure assessment for the unidentified metabolites LM1, LM2, LM3, LM4, LM5 and LM6;<br>((4)) the relevance of the metabolites MT1 (N-tert-butyl-6-chloro-1,3,5-triazine-2,4-diamine), MT 13 (4-(tert-butylamino)-6-(ethylamino)-1,3,5-triazin-2-ol or 6-hydroxy-N2-ethyl-N4-tert-butyl-1,3,5-triazine-2,4-diamine), MT14 (4-amino-6-(tert-butylamino)-1,3,5-triazin-2-ol or N-tert-butyl-6-hydroxy-1,3,5-triazine-2,4-diamine), and of the unidentified metabolites LM1, LM2, LM3, LM4, LM5 and LM6 with respect to cancer, if terbuthylazine is classified under Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 as ‘suspected of causing cancer’.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in points (1) and (2) by 30 June 2012, the information set out in point (3) by 30 June 2013 and the information set out in point (4) within six months from the notification of the classification decision concerning that substance.</td></tr>
<tr><td>17</td><td>Triazoxide<br>CAS No 72459-58-6<br>CIPAC No 729</td><td>7-chloro-3-imidazol-1-yl-1,2,4-benzotriazine 1-oxide</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>toluene: not more than 3 g/kg</td><td>1 October 2011</td><td>30 September 2021</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide for use as seed treatment may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on triazoxide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 17 June 2011, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and workers and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to granivorous birds and shall ensure that conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the long-term risk to granivorous mammals by 30 September 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>18</td><td>8-hydroxyquinoline<br>CAS No<br>148-24-3 (8-hydroxyquinoline)<br>CIPAC No 677<br>(8-hydroxyquinoline)</td><td>8-quinolinol</td><td>≥ 990 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2021</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide and bactericide in greenhouses may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 8-hydroxyquinoline, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 15 July 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the operator safety and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information on 8-hydroxyquinoline and its salts as regards:<br>((1)) the method of analysis for air;<br>((2)) a new storage stability covering the storage time periods of samples from both the metabolism study and from the supervised reidue trials.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority such information by 31 December 2013.</td></tr>
<tr><td>19</td><td>Acrinathrin<br>CAS No 101007-06-1<br>CIPAC No 678</td><td>( <em>S</em> )-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (Z)-(1 <em>R</em> ,3 <em>S</em> )-2,2-dimethyl-3-[2-(2,2,2-trifluoro-1-trifluoromethylethoxycarbonyl)vinyl]cyclopropanecarboxylate or<br>( <em>S</em> )-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl ( <em>Z</em> )-(1 <em>R</em> )- <em>cis</em> -2,2-dimethyl-3-[2-(2,2,2-trifluoro-1-trifluoromethylethoxycarbonyl)vinyl]cyclopropanecarboxylate</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>1,3-dicyclohexylurea: not more than 2 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as insecticide and acaricide may be authorised at rates not exceeding 22,5 g/ha per application.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on acrinathrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 15 July 2011 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and workers and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms, in particular fish, and shall ensure that conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate;<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to non-target arthropods and bees and shall ensure that conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the potential risk to groundwater from the metabolite 3-PBAld;<br>((2)) the chronic risk to fish;<br>((3)) the risk assessment for non-target arthropods;<br>((4)) the possible impact on the worker, the consumer and the environmental risk assessment of the potential stereo-selective degradation of each isomer in plant, animals and the environment.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in points 1, 2 and 3 by 31 December 2013 and the information set out in point 4 2 years after the adoption of specific guidance.</td></tr>
<tr><td>20</td><td>Prochloraz<br>CAS No 67747-09-5<br>CIPAC No 407</td><td><em>N</em> -propyl- <em>N</em> -[2-(2,4,6-trichlorophenoxy)ethyl]imidazole-1-carboxamide</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Sum of dioxins and furans (WHO-PCDD/T TEQ): not more than 0,01 mg/kg</td><td>1 January 2012</td><td>31 December 2023</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as fungicide may be authorised. In the case of outdoor uses, rates shall not exceed 450 g/ha per application.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on prochloraz, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 27 September 2011, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and workers and shall ensure that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms, and shall ensure that conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate;<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the long-term risk to mammals and shall ensure that conditions of authorisation include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicants shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) comparison and verification of the test material used in the mammalian toxicity and ecotoxicity dossiers against the specification of the technical material;<br>((2)) the environmental risk assessment for the metal complexes of prochloraz;<br>((3)) the potential endocrine disrupting properties of prochloraz on birds.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in points 1 and 2 by 31 December 2013 and the information set out in point 3 within 2 years after the adoption of the pertinent OECD test guidelines on endocrine disruption.</td></tr>
<tr><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>22</td><td>Metam<br>CAS No 144-54-7<br>CIPAC No 20</td><td>Methyldithiocarbamic acid</td><td>≥ 965 g/kg<br>Expressed as metam-sodium on a dry weight basis<br>≥ 990 g/kg<br>Expressed as metam-potassium on a dry weight basis<br>Relevant impurities:<br>methylisothiocyanate (MITC)<br>max. 12 g/kg on dry weight basis (metam-sodium),<br>max. 0,42 g/kg on dry weight basis (metam-potassium).<br><em>N,N’</em> -dimethylthiourea (DMTU)<br>max. 23 g/kg on a dry weight basis (metam-sodium),<br>max. 6 g/kg on a dry weight basis (metam-potassium).</td><td>1 July 2012</td><td>30 June 2022</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as nematicide, fungicide, herbicide and insecticide may be authorised for application as soil fumigant prior to planting, limited to one application every third year on the same field.<br>The application may be authorised in open field by soil injection or drip irrigation, and in greenhouse by drip irrigation only. The use of gas-tight plastic film for drip irrigation shall be prescribed.<br>The maximum application rate shall be 153 kg/ha (corresponding to 86,3 kg/ha of MITC) in case of open field applications.<br>Authorisations shall be limited to professional users.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on metam, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 9 March 2012, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States:<br>((a)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and shall ensure that the conditions of use include risk mitigation measures such as application of adequate personal protective equipment and a limitation in the daily work rate;<br>((b)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of workers and shall ensure that the conditions of use include risk mitigation measures, such as use of adequate personal protective equipment, re-entry period and limitation in the daily work rate;<br>((c)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of bystanders and residents and shall ensure that the conditions of use include risk mitigation measures, such as an appropriate buffer zone during and until 24 hours after the application from the perimeter of the application area to any occupied residences and areas used by the general public with obligation to use warning signs and ground markers;<br>((d)) shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions and shall ensure that the conditions of use include risk mitigation measures, such as appropriate buffer zone;<br>((e)) shall pay particular attention to the risk to non-target organisms and shall ensure that conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information on methyl isothiocyanate as regards:<br>((1)) the assessment of the long-range atmospheric transport potential and related environmental risks;<br>((2)) the potential groundwater contamination.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority such information by 31 May 2014.</td></tr>
<tr><td>23</td><td>Bifenthrin<br>CAS No<br>82657-04-3<br>CIPAC No 415</td><td>2-methylbiphenyl-3-ylmethyl (1RS,3RS)-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoroprop-1-enyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate<br>or<br>2-methylbiphenyl-3-ylmethyl (1RS)-cis-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoroprop-1-enyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate</td><td>≥ 930 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Toluene: not more than 5 g/kg</td><td>1 August 2012</td><td>31 July 2019</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses as insecticide in greenhouses with a permanent structure may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on bifenthrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States must pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) releases from greenhouses, such as condensation water, drain water, soil or artificial substrate, in order to preclude risks to aquatic and other non-target organisms;<br>((b)) the protection of pollinator colonies purposely placed in the greenhouse;<br>((c)) the protection of operators and workers, ensuring that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures and provide for adequate labelling of plant protection products.</td></tr>
<tr><td>24</td><td>Fluxapyroxad<br>CAS No 907204-31-3<br>CIPAC No 828</td><td>3-(difluoromethyl)-1-methyl- <em>N</em> -(3′,4′,5′-trifluorobiphenyl-2-yl)pyrazole-4-carboxamide</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg<br>The impurity toluene must not exceed 1 g/kg in the technical material</td><td>1 January 2013</td><td>31 December 2022</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on fluxapyroxad, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 1 June 2012 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to groundwater, if the active substance is applied under vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The purity given in this entry is based on a pilot plant production. The examining Member State shall inform the Commission in accordance with Article 38 of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009 on the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured.</td></tr>

on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 28 September 2012 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to aquatic <tr><td>41</td><td>Spiromesifen<br>CAS No 283594-90-1<br>CIPAC No 747</td><td>3-mesityl-2-oxo-1-oxaspiro[4.4]non-3-en-4-yl 3,3-dimethylbutyrate</td><td>≥ 965 g/kg (racemic)<br>The impurity N,N-dimethylacetamide is of toxicological relevance and must not exceed 4 g/kg in the technical material.</td><td>1 October 2013</td><td>30 September 2023</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on spiromesifen, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 15 March 2013, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the long-term risk to aquatic invertebrates,<br>the risk to pollinating hymenoptera and non-target arthropods if exposure is not negligible,<br>the protection of workers and operators.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards the recalculation of the predicted concentration in groundwater (PECGW) with a FOCUS GW scenario adapted to the supported uses using a Q10 value of 2,58.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority that information by 30 September 2015.</td></tr>

<tr><td>44</td><td>Maltodextrin<br>CAS No 9050-36-6<br>CIPAC No 801</td><td>None allocated</td><td>≥ 910 g/kg</td><td>1 October 2013</td><td>30 September 2023</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on maltodextrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 15 March 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the potential increased growth of fungi and possible presence of mycotoxins on the surface of treated fruits;<br>((b)) the potential risk to honeybees and non-target arthropods.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>

the conclusions of the review report on penthiopyrad, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 3 October 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the protection of operators and workers;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic and soil organisms;<br>((c)) the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((d)) the level of residues in rotational crops following consecutive application of the active substance over several years.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the non-relevance of metabolite M11 (3-methyl-1-{3-[(1-methyl-3-trifluoromethyl-1H-pyrazole-4-carbonyl)amino]thiophen-2-yl}pentanoic acid) for groundwater with the exception of evidence related to the risk of carcinogenicity, which is dependent on the classificationof the parent and specified separately at (3) below;<br>((2)) the toxicological profile and the reference values of the metabolite PAM;<br>((3)) the relevance of the metabolites M11 (3-methyl-1-{3-[(1-methyl-3-trifluoromethyl-1H-pyrazole-4-carbonyl)amino]thiophen-2-yl}pentanoic acid), DM-PCA (3-trifluoromethyl-1H-pyrazole-4- carboxylic acid), PAM (1-methyl-3-trifluoromethyl-1H-pyrazole-4-carboxamide) and PCA (1-methyl-3-trifluoromethyl-1H-pyrazole-4-carboxylic acid) and their risk to contaminate groundwater, if penthiopyrad is classified under Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 as carcinogenic cat. 2.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information set out in points (1) and (2) by 30 April 2016 and the information set out in point (3) within six months from the notification of the classification decision concerning penthiopyrad.</td></tr>
<tr><td>58</td><td>Benalaxyl-M<br>CAS No 98243-83-5<br>CIPAC No 766</td><td>Methyl <em>N</em> -(phenylacetyl)- <em>N</em> -(2,6-xylyl)-D-alaninate</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg</td><td>1 May 2014</td><td>30 April 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on benalaxyl-M, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 3 October 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of workers at re-entry,<br>the risk to groundwater from the metabolites BM-M2 (N-(malonyl)-N-(2,6-xylyl)-DL-alanine) and BM-M3 (N-(malonyl)-N-(2,6-xylyl)-D-alanine), when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>

<tr><td>60</td><td>Spirotetramat<br>CAS No 203313-25-1<br>CIPAC No 795</td><td><em>cis</em> -4-(ethoxycarbonyloxy)-8-methoxy-3-(2,5-xylyl)-1-azaspiro[4.5]dec-3-en-2-one</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg</td><td>1 May 2014</td><td>30 April 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on spirotetramat, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 3 October 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to insectivorous birds.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards the potential for endocrine disruptor effects in birds and fish to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority within two years after the adoption of the OECD test guidelines on endocrine disruption or, alternatively, of Community agreed test guidelines.</td></tr>
<tr><td>61</td><td>Pyroxsulam<br>CAS No 422556-08-9<br>CIPAC No 793</td><td><em>N</em> -(5,7-dimethoxy[1,2,4]triazolo[1,5- <em>a</em> ]pyrimidin-2-yl)-2-methoxy-4-(trifluoromethyl)pyridine-3-sulfonamide</td><td>≥ 965 g/kg</td><td>1 May 2014</td><td>30 April 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pyroxsulam, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 3 October 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to groundwater, when the active substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil or climatic conditions;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the toxicological relevance of impurity number 3 (as referred to in the review report);<br>((2)) the acute toxicity of the metabolite PSA;<br>((3)) the toxicological relevance of metabolite 6-Cl-7-OH-XDE-742.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, Member States and the Authority that information by 30 April 2016.</td></tr>

<tr><td>63</td><td>Sodium silver thiosulfate<br>CAS No not allocated<br>CIPAC No 762</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>≥ 10,0 g Ag/kg<br>Expressed as silver (Ag)</td><td>1 May 2014</td><td>30 April 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only indoor uses in non-edible crops shall be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on sodium silver thiosulfate, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 3 October 2013 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to<br>((a)) the protection of operators and workers;<br>((b)) limiting the possible release of silver ions through disposal of used solutions;<br>((c)) the risk to terrestric vertebrates and soil invertebrates from the use of sewage sludge in agriculture.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>64</td><td>Pyridalyl<br>CAS No 179101-81-6<br>CIPAC No 792</td><td>2,6-dichloro-4-(3,3-dichloroallyloxy)phenyl 3-[5-(trifluoromethyl)-2-pyridyloxy]propyl ether</td><td>≥ 910 g/kg</td><td>1 July 2014</td><td>30 June 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Only uses in greenhouses with permanent structure may be authorised.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pyridalyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 13 December 2013, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to re-entry workers;<br>((b)) the risk to groundwater when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soils and/or climatic conditions;<br>((c)) the risk to birds, mammals and aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the toxicological and ecotoxicological information to address the relevance of impurities 4, 13, 16, 22 and 23;<br>((2)) the relevance of the metabolite HTFP and, concerning that metabolite, the groundwater risk assessment for all uses on crops in greenhouse;<br>((3)) the risk to aquatic invertebrates.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information as regards point (1) by 31 December 2014 and information as regards point (2) and (3) by 30 June 2016.<br>The applicant shall present to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority a monitoring programme to assess the potential groundwater contamination from the metabolite HTFP in vulnerable zones by 30 June 2016. The results of that monitoring programme shall be submitted as a monitoring report to the rapporteur Member State, the Commission and the Authority by 30 June 2018.</td></tr>
<tr><td>65</td><td>S-abscisic acid<br>CAS No<br>21293-29-8<br>CIPAC No<br>Not allocated</td><td>(2Z,4E)-5-[(1S)-1-hydroxy-2,6,6-trimethyl-4-oxocyclohex-2-en-1-yl]-3-methylpenta-2,4-dienoic acid<br>or<br>(7E,9Z)-(6S)-6-hydroxy-3-oxo-11-apo-ε-caroten-11-oic acid</td><td>960  g/kg</td><td>1 July 2014</td><td>30 June 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on S-abscisic acid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 13 December 2013, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>

The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority this monitoring programme by 30 June 2016 and the results in form of a monitoring report by 30 June 2018.</td></tr>

<tr><td>73</td><td>Ipconazole<br>CAS No<br>125225-28-7 (mixture of diastereoisomers)<br>115850-69-6 (ipconazole cc, cis isomer)<br>115937-89-8 (ipconazole ct, trans isomer)<br>CIPAC No 798</td><td>(1 <em>RS</em> ,2 <em>SR</em> ,5 <em>RS</em> ;1 <em>RS</em> ,2 <em>SR</em> ,5 <em>SR</em> )-2-(4-chlorobenzyl)-5-isopropyl-1-(1 <em>H</em> -1,2,4-triazol-1-ylmethyl) cyclopentanol</td><td>≥ 955 g/kg<br>Ipconazole cc: 875 – 930 g/kg<br>Ipconazole ct: 65 – 95 g/kg</td><td>1 September 2014</td><td>31 August 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on ipconazole, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 20 March 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>1.. the risk to granivorous birds;<br>2.. the protection of workers and operators;<br>3.. the risk to fish.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the acceptability of the long-term risk to granivorous birds;<br>((b)) the acceptability of the risk to soil macro-organisms;<br>((c)) the risk of enantio-selective metabolisation or degradation;<br>((d)) the potential endocrine disrupting properties of ipconazole for birds and fish.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information under (a) and (b) by 31 August 2016, the information under (c) within two years after adoption of the pertinent guidance document on evaluation of isomer mixtures and the information under (d) within two years after the adoption of the OECD test guidelines on endocrine disruption or, alternatively, of test guidelines agreed at EU level.</td></tr>
<tr><td>74</td><td>Flubendiamide<br>CAS No 272451-65-7<br>CIPAC No 788</td><td>3-iodo- <em>N</em> '-(2-mesyl-1,1-dimethylethyl)- <em>N</em> -{4-[1,2,2,2-tetrafluoro-1-(trifluoromethyl)ethyl]-o-tolyl}phthalamide</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg</td><td>1 September 2014</td><td>31 August 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on flubendiamide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 20 March 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to aquatic invertebrates;<br>((b)) the potential presence of residues in rotational crops.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>75</td><td><em>Bacillus pumilus</em> QST 2808<br>USDA Agricultural Research Service (NRRL) Patent culture collection in Peoria Illinois, USA under the reference number B-30087</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>≥ 1 × 10 <sup> 12 </sup> CFU/kg</td><td>1 September 2014</td><td>31 August 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Bacillus pumilus</em> QST 2808, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 20 March 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>Bacillus pumilus</em> QST 2808 is to be considered as a potential sensitizer.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the identification of the aminosugar produced by <em>Bacillus pumilus</em> QST 2808;<br>((b)) analytical data for the content of that aminosugar in the production batches.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 31 August 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>76</td><td>Metobromuron<br>CAS No 3060-89-7<br>CIPAC No 168</td><td>3-(4-bromophenyl)-1-methoxy-1-methylurea</td><td>≥ 978 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2015</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on metobromuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 11 July 2014, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the protection of workers and operators;<br>((b)) the risk to birds, mammals, aquatic organisms and terrestrial non-target plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the toxicological assessment of the metabolites CGA 18236, CGA 18237, CGA 18238 and 4-bromoaniline;<br>((b)) the acceptability of the long-term risk to birds and mammals.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 31 December 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>77</td><td>Aminopyralid<br>CAS No 150114-71-9<br>CIPAC No 771</td><td>4-amino-3,6-dichloropyridine-2-carboxylic acid</td><td>≥ 920 g/kg<br>The following relevant impurity shall not exceed a certain threshold:<br>Picloram ≤ 40 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2015</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on aminopyralid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 11 July 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to groundwater, if the substance is applied under vulnerable soil or climatic conditions;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic macrophytes and terrestrial non-target plants;<br>((c)) chronic risk to fish.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>78</td><td>Metaflumizone<br>CAS No 139968-49-3<br>CIPAC No 779</td><td>(EZ)-2′-[2-(4-cyanophenyl)-1-(α,α,α-trifluoro-m-tolyl)ethylidene]-4-(trifluoromethoxy)carbanilohydrazide</td><td>≥ 945 g/kg<br>(90-100 % E-isomer<br>10-0 % Z-isomer)<br>The following relevant impurities shall not exceed a certain threshold:<br>Hydrazine ≤ 1 mg/kg<br>4-(trifluoromethoxy)phenyl isocyanate ≤ 100 mg/kg<br>Toluene ≤ 2 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2015</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on metaflumizone, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 11 July 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to fish and sediment dwelling organisms;<br>((b)) the risk to snail- or earthworm-eating birds.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the equivalence of the material used in the toxicological and ecotoxicological studies with the proposed technical specification;<br>((2)) information addressing the potential of metaflumizone for bioaccumulation in aquatic organisms and biomagnification in aquatic food chains.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information requested under (1) by 30 June 2015 and under (2) by 31 December 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>79</td><td><em>Streptomyces lydicus</em> strain WYEC 108<br>Collection number: American Type Culture Collection (USDA)<br>ATCC 55445</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Minimum concentration: 5,0 × 10 <sup> 8 </sup> CFU/g</td><td>1 January 2015</td><td>31 December 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Streptomyces lydicus</em> strain WYEC 108, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 11 July 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to aquatic organisms;<br>((b)) the risk to soil dwelling organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>80</td><td>Meptyldinocap<br>CAS No 6119-92-2<br>CIPAC No 811</td><td>Mixture of 75-100 % ( <em>RS</em> )-2-(1-methylheptyl)-4,6-dinitrophenyl crotonate and 25-– 0 % ( <em>RS</em> )-2-(1-methylheptyl)-4,6-dinitrophenyl isocrotonate</td><td>≥ 900 g/kg (mixture of <em>trans-</em> and <em>cis-</em> isomers with a defined ratio range of 25:1 to 20:1)<br>Relevant impurity:<br>2,6-dinitro-4-[(4 <em>RS</em> )-octan-4-yl]phenyl (2 <em>E/Z</em> )-but-2-enoate<br>max content 0,4 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2015</td><td>31 March 2015</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on meptyldinocap, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on the Food Chain and Animal Health on 16 May 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to operators;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic invertebrates.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the groundwater exposure assessment for metabolites (3 <em>RS</em> )-3-(2-hydroxy-3,5-dinitro-phenyl)-butanoic acid (X103317) and (2 <em>RS</em> )-2-(2-hydroxy-3,5-dinitro-phenyl)-propionic acid (X12335709);<br>((b)) the possible impact of any preferential degradation and/or conversion of the mixture of isomers on the worker risk assessment, the consumer risk assessment and the environment.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information set out in point (a) by 31 March 2017 and the information set out in point (b) two years after the adoption of specific guidance by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td>81</td><td>Chromafenozide<br>CAS No 143807-66-3<br>CIPAC No 775</td><td><em>N′-tert</em> -butyl-5-methyl-N′-(3,5-xyloyl)chromane-6-carbohydrazide</td><td>≥ 935 g/kg<br>The following relevant impurity must not exceed a certain threshold in the technical material:<br>Butyl acetate (n-buthyl acetate, CAS No 123-86-4): ≤ 8 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2015</td><td>31 March 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on chromafenozide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 10 October 2014, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to groundwater, if the substance is applied under vulnerable soil or climatic conditions;<br>((b)) the risk to non-target Lepidoptera in off-crop areas;<br>((c)) the risk to sediment-dwelling organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the non-significance of the difference between the material used for ecotoxicological testing and the agreed specification of the technical material for the risk assessment;<br>((2)) the assessment of the risk to sediment dwelling organisms from metabolite M-010;<br>((3)) the leaching potential of metabolites M-006 and M-023 to groundwater.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information requested under (1) by 30 September 2015 and under (2) and (3) by 31 March 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>82</td><td>Gamma-cyhalothrin<br>CAS No 76703-62-3<br>CIPAC No 768</td><td>( <em>S</em> )-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1 <em>R</em> ,3 <em>R</em> )-3-[( <em>Z</em> )-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate or<br>( <em>S</em> )-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1 <em>R</em> )- <em>cis</em> -3-[( <em>Z</em> )-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2015</td><td>31 March 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on gamma-cyhalothrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 10 October 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the safety of operators and workers;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) analytical methods for the monitoring of residues in body fluids, tissues and environmental matrices;<br>((2)) the toxicity profile of the metabolites CPCA, PBA and PBA(OH);<br>((3)) the long-term risk to wild mammals;<br>((4)) the potential for biomagnification in terrestrial and aquatic food chains.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information by 31 March 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>83</td><td><em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> subsp. <em>plantarum</em> strain D747<br>Accession number in the Agricultural Research Culture Collection (NRRL), Peoria, Illinois, USA: B-50405<br>Deposit number in the International Patent Organism Depositary, Tokyo, Japan: FERM BP-8234.</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Minimum concentration: 2,0 × 10 <sup> 11 </sup> CFU/g</td><td>1 April 2015</td><td>31 March 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> subsp. <em>plantarum</em> strain D747, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 10 October 2014 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> subsp. <em>plantarum</em> strain D747 is to be considered as a potential sensitizer. Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>Strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process shall be assured by the producer.</td></tr>
<tr><td>84</td><td>Terpenoid blend QRD 460<br>CIPAC No: 982</td><td>Terpenoid blend QRD 460 is a blend of three components:<br>α-terpinene: 1-isopropyl-4-methylcyclohexa-1,3-diene;<br><em>p</em> -cymene: 1-isopropyl-4-methylbenzene;<br><em>d</em> -limonene: ( <em>R</em> )-4-isopropenyl-1-methylcyclohexene.</td><td>The nominal concentration of each component in the active substance as manufactured should be as follows:<br>α-terpinene: 59,7 %;<br><em>p</em> -cymene: 22,4 %;<br><em>d</em> -limonene: 17,9 %.<br>Each component should have a minimum purity as follows:<br>α-terpinene: 89 %;<br><em>p</em> -cymene: 97 %;<br><em>d</em> -limonene: 93 %.</td><td>10 August 2015</td><td>10 August 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on terpenoid blend QRD-460, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the stability of formulations on storage;<br>((b)) the protection of operators and workers, ensuring that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((c)) the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((d)) the protection of surface water and aquatic organisms;<br>((e)) the protection of bees and non-target arthropods.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (5 batch analysis for the blend should be provided), supported by acceptable and validated methods of analysis. It should be confirmed that there are no relevant impurities present in the technical material;<br>((2)) the equivalence of the material used in the toxicological and ecotoxicological studies with the confirmed technical specification.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 10 February 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>85</td><td>Fenhexamid<br>CAS No: 126833-17-8<br>CIPAC No: 603</td><td>N-(2,3-dichloro-4-hydroxyphenyl)-1-methylcyclohexane-1-carboxamide</td><td>≥ 975 g/kg<br>The following relevant impurity must not exceed a certain threshold in the technical material:<br>toluene: max. 1 g/kg,<br>4-amino-2,3-dichlorophenol: max. 3 g/kg.</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2030</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on fenhexamid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators during field crop handheld operations,<br>the protection of workers re-entering indoor-treated crops,<br>the risk to aquatic organisms,<br>the long-term risk to mammals for field uses.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>

The relevance of impurities present in the technical material should be confirmed,<br>The compliance of the toxicity batches with the technical specification.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 5 February 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>87</td><td>Pyridate<br>CAS No: 55512-33-9<br>CIPAC No: 447</td><td>O-6-chloro-3-phenylpyridazin-4-yl S-octyl thiocarbonate</td><td>≥ 900 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2030</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pyridate, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms, non-target terrestrial plants, and herbivorous mammals.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>88</td><td>Sulfoxaflor<br>CAS No: 946578-00-3<br>CIPAC No: 820</td><td>[methyl(oxo){1-[6-(trifluoromethyl)-3-pyridyl]ethyl}-λ <sup> 6 </sup> -sulfanylidene]cyanamide</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg</td><td>18 August 2015</td><td>18 August 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on sulfoxaflor, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to bees and other non-target arthropods;<br>((b)) the risk to bees and bumble bees released for pollination, when the substance is applied in glasshouses.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) the risk to honey bees via the different routes of exposure, in particular nectar, pollen, guttation fluid and dust;<br>((b)) risk to honey bees foraging in nectar or pollen in succeeding crops and flowering weeds;<br>((c)) the risk to pollinators other than honey bees;<br>((d)) the risk to bee brood.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 18 August 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>89</td><td>Sulfosulfuron<br>CAS No: 141776-32-1<br>CIPAC No: 601</td><td>1-(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-yl)-3-(2-ethylsulfonylimidazo[1,2-a]pyridine-3-ylsulfonyl)urea</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg<br>The following relevant impurity must not exceed a certain threshold in the technical material:<br>Phenol: &lt; 2 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2030</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on sulfosulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>the risk to soil non-target macro-organisms other than earthworms, non-target terrestrial plants and aquatic organisms.</td></tr>
<tr><td>90</td><td>Florasulam<br>CAS No 145701-23-1<br>CIPAC No 616</td><td>2′,6′,8-trifluoro-5-methoxy[1,2,4]triazolo[1,5- <em>c</em> ]pyrimidine-2-sulfonanilide</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Impurity: 2,6-DFA, not more than 2 g/kg</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2030</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on florasulam, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms and non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>91</td><td>Flupyradifurone<br>CAS No: 951659-40-8<br>CIPAC No: 987</td><td>4-[(6-chloro-3-pyridylmethyl)(2,2-difluoroethyl) amino]furan-2(5H)-one</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg</td><td>9 December 2015</td><td>9 December 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on flupyradifurone, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of workers and operators,<br>the risk to non-target arthropods, aquatic invertebrates and small herbivorous mammals,<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions,<br>residues in animal matrices and rotational crops.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production) including the relevance of some individual impurities,<br>((2)) the compliance of the toxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification,<br>((3)) the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information requested under point (1) and (2) by 9 June 2016, the information requested under point (3) within 2 years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
