The purity range for each isomer shall be 337,5 g/kg to 412,5 g/kg.</td><td>18 December 2015</td><td>18 December 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on rescalure, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.</td></tr>
<tr><td>93</td><td>Mandestrobin<br>CAS No: 173662-97-0<br>CIPAC No: Not available</td><td>( <em>RS</em> )-2-methoxy- <em>N</em> -methyl-2-[α-(2,5-xylyloxy)- <em>o</em> -tolyl]acetamide</td><td>≥ 940 g/kg (on a dry weight basis)<br>Xylenes (ortho, meta, para), ethyl benzene max. 5 g/kg (TK)</td><td>9 December 2015</td><td>9 December 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on mandestrobin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to aquatic organisms,<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production) including the relevance of some individual impurities;<br>((2)) the compliance of the toxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 9 June 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>94</td><td>2,4-D<br>CAS No: 94-75-7<br>CIPAC No: 1</td><td>(2,4-dichlorophenoxy) acetic acid</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Free phenols (expressed as 2,4-DCP): not more than 3 g/kg.<br>Sum of dioxins and furans (WHO-TCDD TEQ): not more than 0,01 mg/kg.</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2030</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 2,4-D, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms, terrestrial organisms and consumers in cases of uses above 750 g/ha.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The notifier shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority:<br>((1)) confirmatory information in the form of the submission of the complete study results from the existing extended one-generation study;<br>((2)) confirmatory information in the form of the submission of the Amphibian Metamorphosis Assay (AMA) (OECD (2009) Test No 231) as to verify the potential endocrine properties of the substance.<br>The information set out in point (1) shall be submitted by 4 June 2016 and the information set out in point (2) by 4 December 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>95</td><td>Pyraflufen-ethyl<br>CAS No 129630-19-9<br>CIPAC No 605.202</td><td>ethyl [2-chloro-5-(4-chloro-5-difluoromethoxy-1-methylpyrazol-3-yl)-4-fluorophenoxy]acetate</td><td>≥ 956 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2016</td><td>31 March 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pyraflufen-ethyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of aquatic organisms,<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>96</td><td>Iprovalicarb<br>CAS No 140923-17-7<br>CIPAC No 620</td><td>isopropyl [(1S)-2-methyl-1-{[(1RS)-1-p-tolylethyl]carbamoyl}propyl]carbamate</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Toluene: not more than 3 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2016</td><td>31 March 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on iprovalicarb, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater from the relevant soil metabolite PMPA when the active substance is applied in regions with low clay containing soil types,<br>the safety of operators and workers,<br>the protection of aquatic organisms in the case of formulated products containing other active substances.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority, confirmatory information as regards the genotoxic potential of soil metabolite PMPA. This information shall be submitted by 30 September 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>97</td><td>Pinoxaden<br>CAS No 243973-20-8<br>CIPAC No 776</td><td>8-(2,6-diethyl-p-tolyl)-1,2,4,5-tetrahydro-7-oxo-7H-pyrazolo[1,2-d][1,4,5]oxadiazepin-9-yl 2,2-dimethylpropionate</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>Toluene max. content 1 g/kg</td><td>1 July 2016</td><td>30 June 2026</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pinoxaden, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, as finalised in the Standing Committee on Plants, Animals, Food and Feed on 29 January 2016 shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>The Member States concerned shall carry out monitoring programmes to verify potential groundwater contamination from the metabolite M2 in vulnerable zones, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((a)) a validated method of analysis of metabolites M11, M52, M54, M55 and M56 in ground water;<br>((b)) the relevance of the metabolites M3, M11, M52, M54, M55 and M56, and the corresponding groundwater risk assessment, if pinoxaden is classified under Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 as H361d (suspected of damaging the unborn child).<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information set out in point (a) by 30 June 2018 and the information set out in point (b) within six months from the notification of the classification decision under Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 concerning pinoxaden.</td></tr>
<tr><td>98</td><td>Acibenzolar-S-methyl<br>CAS No 135158-54-2<br>CIPAC No 597</td><td>S-methyl benzo[1,2,3]thiadiazole-7-carbothioate</td><td>970 g/kg<br>Toluene: max. 5 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2016</td><td>31 March 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on acibenzolar-S-methyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk for consumers via food intake;<br>((b)) the protection of operators and workers;<br>((c)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall by 1 June 2017 submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority, confirmatory information as regards the relevance and reproducibility of the morphometric changes observed in the cerebellum of foetuses linked to exposure to acibenzolar-S-methyl and whether these changes may be produced via an endocrine mode of action. The information to be submitted shall includea systematic review of the available evidence assessed on the basis of available guidance (e.g. EFSA GD on Systematic Review methodology, 2010).</td></tr>
<tr><td>99</td><td>Cyantraniliprole<br>CAS No:<br>736994-63-1<br>CIPAC No: Not allocated.</td><td>3-bromo-1-(3-chloro-2-pyridyl)-4′-cyano-2′-methyl-6′-(methylcarbamoyl)pyrazole-5-carboxanilide</td><td>≥ 940 g/kg<br>IN-Q6S09 max. 1 mg/kg<br>IN-RYA13 max. 20 mg/kg<br>methanesulfonic acid max. 2 g/kg<br>acetonitrile max. 2 g/kg<br>heptane max. 7 g/kg<br>3-picoline max. 3 g/kg.</td><td>14 September 2016</td><td>14 September 2026</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on cyantraniliprole, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the risk to operators;<br>((b)) the risk to aquatic organisms, bees and other non-target arthropods;<br>((c)) the risk to bees and bumble bees released for pollination, when the substance is applied in glasshouses;<br>((d)) the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater are abstracted for drinking water within 2 years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>100</td><td>Isofetamid<br>CAS No: 875915-78-9<br>CIPAC No: 972</td><td><em>N</em> -[1,1-dimethyl-2-(4-isopropoxy-o-tolyl)-2-oxoethyl]-3-methylthiophene-2-carboxamide</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg</td><td>15 September 2016</td><td>15 September 2026</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on isofetamid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to operators, workers and aquatic organisms, in particular fish.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production) including the relevance of impurities;<br>((2)) the compliance of the toxicity and ecotoxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification;<br>((3)) the effect of water treatment process chlorination on the nature of residues, including the potential for the formation of chlorinated residues that may be formed from residues present in surface water, when surface water is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the information requested under points (1) and (2) by 15 March 2017 and the information requested under point (3) within 2 years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>101</td><td><em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> strain MBI 600.<br>Accession number in the National Collection of Industrial, Marine and Food Bacteria Ltd (NCIMB), Scotland: NCIMB 12376<br>Deposit number in the American Type Culture Collection (ATCC): SD-1414</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Minimum concentration:<br>5,0 × 10<sup> 14 </sup> CFU/kg</td><td>16 September 2016</td><td>16 September 2026</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> strain MBI 600, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured, including full characterisation of impurities and metabolites;<br>((b)) the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>Bacillus amyloliquefaciens</em> strain MBI 600 is to be considered as a potential sensitiser.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>Strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process shall be assured by the producer.</td></tr>
<tr><td>102</td><td>Ethofumesate<br>CAS No 26225-79-6<br>CIPAC No 233</td><td>(RS)-2-ethoxy-2,3-dihydro-3,3-dimethylbenzofuran-5-yl methanesulfonate</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>The following impurities are of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following levels in the technical material:<br>EMS; ethyl methane sulfonate: maximum of 0,1 mg/kg<br>iBMS; iso-butyl methane sulfonate: maximum of 0,1 mg/kg</td><td>1 November 2016</td><td>31 October 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on ethofumesate, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>103</td><td>Picolinafen<br>CAS No 137641-05-5<br>CIPAC No 639</td><td>4′-fluoro-6-(α,α,α-trifluoro-m-tolyloxy)pyridine-2-carboxanilide</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg</td><td>1 November 2016</td><td>30 June 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on picolinafen, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the impurities in the technical active substance;<br>the protection of mammals, especially of large herbivorous mammals;<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants;<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>the protection of aquatic organisms, especially to algae.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>104</td><td>Thifensulfuron-methyl<br>CAS No 79277-27-3<br>CIPAC No 452</td><td>methyl 3-(4-methoxy-6-methyl-1,3,5-triazin-2-ylcarbamoylsulfamoyl)thiophene-2-carboxylate</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg</td><td>1 November 2016</td><td>31 October 2031</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on thifensulfuron-methyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater;<br>the protection of non-target plants and aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures and the obligation to monitor the groundwater, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the absence of genotoxicity of metabolites IN-A4098 and its derivative IN-B5528, IN-A5546 and IN-W8268;<br>((2)) mechanistic data to rule out an endocrine mediated mode of action for mammary gland tumours;<br>((3)) the risk to aquatic organisms from thifensulfuron-methyl and metabolite IN-D8858 and the risk to soil organisms from metabolites IN-JZ789 and 2 acid 3 triuret;<br>((4)) the relevance of the metabolites IN-A4098, IN-L9223 and IN-JZ789 if thifensulfuron-methyl is classified as reprotoxic category 2 under Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 and the risk that those metabolites contaminate groundwater.<br>The applicant shall submit the information requested under point (1) by 31 March 2017, under points (2) and (3) by 30 June 2017 and under point (4) within six months after the notification of the classification decision concerning thifensulfuron-methyl.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="2">105</td><td>Thiabendazole</td><td>2-(thiazol-4-yl)benzimidazole</td><td rowspan="2">≥ 985 g/kg</td><td rowspan="2">1 April 2017</td><td rowspan="2">31 March 2032</td><td rowspan="2">For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on thiabendazole, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators and consumers,<br>the protection of groundwater,<br>the control of waste water from post-harvest uses.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit by 31 March 2019 to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information regarding Level 2 tests as currently indicated in the OECD Conceptual Framework investigating the potential for endocrine-mediated effects of thiabendazole.</td></tr>
<tr><td>CAS No 148-79-8<br>CIPAC No 323</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>106</td><td>Oxathiapiprolin<br>CAS No:<br>1003318-67-9<br>CIPAC No: 985</td><td>1-(4-{4-[(5RS)-5-(2,6-difluorophenyl)-4,5-dihydro-1,2-oxazol-3-yl]-1,3-thiazol-2-yl}-1-piperidyl)-2-[5-methyl-3-(trifluoromethyl)-1H-pyrazol-1-yl]ethanone</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg</td><td>3 March 2017</td><td>3 March 2027</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on oxathiapiprolin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production) including the relevance of impurities;<br>((2)) the compliance of the toxicity and ecotoxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification.<br>The applicant shall submit the information requested under points (1) and (2) by 3 September 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>107</td><td>Iodosulfuron<br>CAS No 185119-76-0 (parent)<br>CAS No 144550-36-7 (iodosulfuron-methyl-sodium)<br>CIPAC No 634 (parent)<br>CIPAC No 634.501 (iodosulfuron-methyl-sodium)</td><td>4-iodo-2-[(4-methoxy-6-methyl-1,3,5-triazin-2-yl)carbamoylsulfamoyl]benzoic acid<br>(iodosulfuron)<br>sodium ({[5-iodo-2-(methoxycarbonyl)phenyl]sulfonyl}carbamoyl)(4-methoxy-6-methyl-1,3,5-triazin-2-yl)azanide<br>(iodosulfuron-methyl-sodium)</td><td>≥ 910 g/kg (expressed as iodosulfuron-methyl-sodium)</td><td>1 April 2017</td><td>31 March 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on iodosulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to consumers,<br>the risk to non-target terrestrial plants,<br>the risk to aquatic plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the genotoxic potential of the metabolite triazine-amine (IN-A4098), in order to confirm that this metabolite is not genotoxic and not relevant for the risk assessment;<br>((2)) the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the information requested under point (1) 1 October 2017 and the information requested under point (2) by two years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>108</td><td>Flazasulfuron<br>CAS No 104040-78-0<br>CIPAC No 595</td><td>1-(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-yl)-3-(3-trifluoromethyl-2-pyridylsulphonyl)urea</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg</td><td>1 August 2017</td><td>31 July 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on flazasulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of aquatic plants,<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants,<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in drinking water within a period of two years a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater being made public by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td>109</td><td><em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain NPP111B005<br>Accession number in the CNCM (Collection Nationale de Culture de Microorganismes) — Institut Pasteur, Paris, France: I-2961.</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Max. level of beauvericin 24 μg/L</td><td>7 June 2017</td><td>7 June 2027</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain NPP111B005, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain NPP111B005 is to be considered, as any micro-organism, as a potential sensitizer, and paying special attention to exposure through inhalation,<br>the maximum level of the metabolite beauvericin in the formulated product.<br>Strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process shall be assured by the producer.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>110</td><td><em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain 147<br>Accession number in the CNCM (Collection nationale de cultures de micro-organismes) — Institut Pasteur, Paris, France: I-2960.</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Max. level of beauvericin: 24 μg/L</td><td>6 June 2017</td><td>6 June 2027</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain 147, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain 147 is to be considered, as any micro-organism, as a potential sensitizer, and paying special attention to exposure through inhalation,<br>the maximum level of the metabolite beauvericin in the formulated product.<br>Strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process shall be assured by the producer.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>111</td><td>Mesosulfuron (parent)<br>Mesosulfuron-methyl (variant)<br>CAS No 208465-21-8<br>(mesosulfuron-methyl)<br>CIPAC No 663<br>(mesosulfuron)<br>CIPAC No 663.201<br>(mesosulfuron-methyl</td><td>Mesosulfuron-methyl:<br>methyl-2-[(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-ylcarbamoyl)sulfamoyl]-α-(methanesulfonamido)- <em>p</em> -toluate<br>Mesosulfuron:<br>2-[(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-ylcarbamoyl)sulfamoyl]-α-methanesulfonamido- <em>p</em> -toluic acid</td><td>≥ 930 g/kg<br>(expressed as mesosulfuron-methyl)</td><td>1 July 2017</td><td>30 June 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on mesosulfuron and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of aquatic organisms and non-target terrestrial plants;<br>the protection of groundwater.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in drinking water within a period of two years of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater being made public by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td>112</td><td>Mesotrione<br>CAS No 104206-82-8<br>CIPAC No 625</td><td>Mesotrione<br>2-(4-mesyl-2-nitrobenzoyl) cyclohexane -1,3-dione</td><td>≥ 920 g/kg<br>R287431 max 2 mg/kg<br>R287432 max 2 g/kg<br>1,2-dichloroethane max 1 g/kg</td><td>1 June 2017</td><td>31 May 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on mesotrione, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators,<br>the protection of groundwater in vulnerable regions,<br>the protection of mammals, aquatic and non-target plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. the genotoxic profile of the metabolite AMBA;<br>2.. the potential endocrine disrupting mode of action of the active substance in particular level 2 and 3 tests, currently indicated in the OECD Conceptual framework (OECD 2012) and analysed in the EFSA Scientific opinion on the hazard assessment of endocrine disruptors;<br>3.. the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater are abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the relevant information requested under point 1 by 1 July 2017 and the relevant information requested under point 2 by 31 December 2017. The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the confirmatory information requested under point 3 within a period of two years after a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater being made public by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td>113</td><td>Cyhalofop-butyl<br>CAS No 122008-85-9<br>CIPAC No 596</td><td>butyl ( <em>R</em> )-2-[4-(4-cyano-2-fluorophenoxy) phenoxy]propionate</td><td>950 g/kg</td><td>1 July 2017</td><td>30 June 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on cyhalofop-butyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators,<br>the technical specification,<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>114</td><td>Propoxycarbazone (parent)<br>Propoxycarbazone-sodium (variant)<br>CAS No 145026-81-9 (propoxycarbazone)<br>CAS No 181274-15-7 (propoxycarbazone-sodium)<br>CIPAC No 655 (propoxycarbazone)<br>CIPAC No 655.011 (propoxycarbazone-sodium)</td><td>Propoxycarbazone:<br>methyl 2-[(4,5-dihydro-4-methyl-5-oxo-3-propoxy-1H-1,2,4-triazole-1-carboxamido)sulfonyl]benzoate<br>Propoxycarbazone-sodium:<br>sodium {[2-(methoxycarbonyl)phenyl]sulfonyl}[(4,5-dihydro-4-methyl-5-oxo-3-propoxy-1H-1,2,4-triazol-1-yl)carbonyl]azanide</td><td>≥ 950g/kg<br><em>(expressed as Propoxycarbazone-sodium)</em></td><td>1 September 2017</td><td>31 August 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on propoxycarbazone, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of aquatic organisms, in particular aquatic plants and of and non-target terrestrial plants,<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in drinking water within a period of 2 years of a guidance document on the evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater being made public by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td>115</td><td>Benzoic acid<br>CAS No 65-85-0<br>CIPAC No 622</td><td>Benzoic acid</td><td>≥ 990 g/kg</td><td>1 September 2017</td><td>31 August 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on benzoic acid and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the protection of operators, ensuring that conditions of use impose the use of adequate personal protective equipment.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>116</td><td>2,4-DB<br>CAS No 94-82-6<br>CIPAC No 83</td><td>4-(2,4-dichlorophenoxy) butyric acid</td><td>≥ 940 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Free phenols (expressed as 2,4-dichlorophenol (2,4-DCP)): max. 15 g/kg.<br>Dibenzo- <em>p</em> -dioxins and polychlorinated dibenzofurans (TCDD toxic equivalents (TEQ)): max. 0,01 mg/kg.</td><td>1 November 2017</td><td>31 October 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 2,4-DB, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators and workers,<br>the protection of consumers from products of animal origin,<br>the protection of wild mammals,<br>the protection of soil non-target organisms,<br>the protection of aquatic organisms,<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>117</td><td>Maleic hydrazide<br>CAS No 123-33-1<br>CIPAC No 310</td><td>6-hydroxy-2H-pyridazin-3-one</td><td>≥ 979 g/kg<br>Until 1 November 2018, the impurity hydrazine shall not exceed 1 mg/kg in the technical material.<br>From 1 November 2018, the impurity hydrazine shall not exceed 0,028 mg/kg in the technical material.</td><td>1 November 2017</td><td>31 October 2032</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on maleic hydrazide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of consumers,<br>the operator and worker safety; the conditions of the use should include the application of adequate personal protective equipment.<br>Member States shall ensure, where appropriate, that the label of the treated crops includes the indication that the crops were treated with maleic hydrazide, and the accompanying instructions to avoid exposure of the livestock.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>118</td><td>Glyphosate<br>CAS No 1071-83-6<br>CIPAC No 284</td><td>N-(phosphonomethyl)glycine</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg<br>Impurities:<br>Formaldehyde, less than 1 g/kg<br><em>N</em> -Nitroso-glyphosate, less than 1 mg/kg</td><td>16 December 2017</td><td>15 December 2022</td><td>Only uses as herbicide may be authorised.<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on glyphosate, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of the groundwater in vulnerable areas, in particular with respect to non-crop uses,<br>the protection of operators and amateur users,<br>the risk to terrestrial vertebrates and non-target terrestrial plants,<br>the risk to diversity and abundance of non-target terrestrial arthropods and vertebrates via trophic interactions,<br>compliance of pre-harvest uses with good agricultural practices.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>Member States shall ensure that use of plant protection products containing glyphosate is minimised in the specific areas listed in Article 12(a) of Directive 2009/128/EC.<br>Member States shall ensure equivalence between the specifications of the technical material, as commercially manufactured, and those of the test material used in the toxicological studies.<br>Member States shall ensure that plant protection products containing glyphosate do not contain the co-formulant POE-tallowamine (CAS No 61791-26-2).</td></tr>
<tr><td>119</td><td>Acetamiprid<br>CAS No 135410-20-7<br>CIPAC No 649</td><td>(E)-N1-[(6-Chloro-3-pyridyl)methyl]-N2-cyano-N1-methylacetamidine</td><td>≥ 990 g/kg</td><td>1 March 2018</td><td>28 February 2033</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on acetamiprid, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to aquatic organisms, bees and other non-target arthropods,<br>the risk to birds and mammals,<br>the risk to consumers,<br>the risk to operators.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>120</td><td>Bentazone<br>CAS No 25057-89-0<br>CIPAC No 366</td><td>3-isopropyl-1 <em>H</em> -2,1,3-benzothiadiazin-4(3 <em>H</em> )-one 2,2- dioxide</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg<br>1,2-dichloroethane &lt; 3 mg/kg</td><td>1 June 2018</td><td>31 May 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on bentazone, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the technical specification,<br>the protection of operators and workers,<br>the risk to birds and mammals,<br>the protection of groundwater, particularly but not only in drinking water protected areas, and shall carefully consider the timing of application, soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit by 1 February 2019 to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards Level 2/3 tests as currently indicated in the OECD Conceptual Framework investigating the potential for endocrine-mediated effects of bentazone.</td></tr>

Measures are required to ensure that such products do not enter the food and feed chain, taking into account the natural background level of beauvericin on cereal grains;<br>the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>B. bassiana</em> strain IMI389521 is to be considered, as any micro-organism, as a potential sensitiser.<br>The compliance with strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process, in order to ensure the fulfilment of the limits on microbiological contamination as referred to in the Working Document SANCO/12116/2012.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>131</td><td><em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain PPRI 5339<br>Accession number in the Agricultural Research Culture Collection (NRRL) International Depositary Authority: NRRL 50757</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>Max. level of beauvericin: 0,5 mg/kg</td><td>20 February 2019</td><td>20 February 2029</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on <em>Beauveria bassiana</em> strain PPRI 5339, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the level of the metabolite beauvericin content in a shelf-life study after storage of the formulation(s) containing <em>B. bassiana</em> strain PPRI 5339;<br>the effects on pollinators introduced in glasshouses following exposure to formulation(s) different from the representative one supporting this approval;<br>the protection of operators and workers, taking into account that <em>B. bassiana</em> strain PPRI 5339 is to be considered, as any micro-organism, as a potential sensitizer.<br>The compliance with strict maintenance of environmental conditions and quality control analysis during the manufacturing process, in order to ensure the fulfilment of the limits on microbiological contamination as referred to in the Working Document SANCO/12116/2012.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures where appropriate.</td></tr>

water;<br>3.. an updated assessment of the information submitted and, where relevant further information, confirming that flutianil is not an endocrine disruptor in accordance with Points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, applying also the ECHA and EFSA guidance for identification of endocrine disruptors.<br>The applicant shall submit the information:<br>referred to in point 1 by 14 April 2020;<br>referred to in point 2 within two years from the date of publication, from the Commission, of a guidance document on the evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater; and<br>referred to in point 3 by 14 April 2021.</td></tr>
<tr><td>134</td><td>Isoxaflutole<br>CAS No 141112-29-0<br>CIPAC No 575</td><td>(5-cyclopropyl-1,2-oxazol-4-yl)(α,α,α-trifluoro-2-mesyl-p-tolyl)methanone</td><td>≥ 972 g/kg</td><td>1 August 2019</td><td>31 July 2034</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on isoxaflutole, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment, Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions,<br>the protection of aquatic organisms, wild mammals and non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water. The applicant shall submit this information within 2 years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on the evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.<br>The applicant shall also provide an updated assessment to confirm that isoxaflutole is not an endocrine disruptor within the meaning of points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II to Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Commission Regulation (EU) 2018/605 and in accordance with the guidance for identification of endocrine disruptors by 10 May 2021.</td></tr>
<tr><td>135</td><td>carvone<br>2244-16-8 (d-carvone = S-carvone = (+)-carvone)<br>Carvone: 602<br>d-carvone: not allocated</td><td>( <em>S</em> )-5-isopropenyl-2-methylcyclohex-2-en-1-one<br>Or<br>(S)-p-mentha-6,8-dien-2-one</td><td>923 g/kg d-carvone</td><td>1 August 2019</td><td>31 July 2034</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on carvone, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators, ensuring that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate. In particular, consideration should be given to the necessary time period before entry into storage rooms after the application of plant protection products containing carvone.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit that information within two years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>136</td><td>1-methylcyclopropene<br>CAS No 3100-04-7<br>CIPAC No 767</td><td>1-methylcyclopropene</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg (technical concentrate)<br>The following impurities are of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following levels in the technical material (technical concentrate):<br>1-chloro-2-methylpropene: maximum of 0,2 g/kg,<br>3-chloro-2-methylpropene: maximum of 0,2 g/kg.<br>For 1-methylcyclopropene generated <em>in situ</em> , Heptane and methylcyclohexane are toxicologically relevant impurities. These impurities should remain below 10 %.</td><td>1 August 2019</td><td>31 July 2034</td><td>Only uses as plant growth regulator for post-harvest storage in sealable warehouse may be authorised.<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on 1-methycyclopropene, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.</td></tr>
<tr><td>137</td><td>Dimethenamid-P<br>CAS No 163515-14-8<br>CIPAC No 638</td><td>(S)-2-chloro-N-(2,4-dimethyl-3-thienyl)-N-(2-methoxy1-methylethyl)acetamide</td><td>≥ 930 g/kg<br>The following impurity is of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following level in the technical material:<br>1,1,1,2-Tetrachloroethane (TCE): ≤ 1,0 g/kg</td><td>1 September 2019</td><td>31 August 2034</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on dimethenamid-P, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators and workers, ensuring that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment;<br>the protection of groundwater, in particular regarding the metabolites of dimethenamid-P;<br>the protection of aquatic organisms and small herbivorous mammals.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or ground water is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the requested information within two years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>138</td><td>Tolclofos-methyl<br>CAS No 57018-04-9<br>CIPAC No 479</td><td>O-2,6-dichloro-p-tolylO, O-dimethyl phosphorothioate<br>O-2,6-dichloro-4-methylphenyl O, O-dimethyl phosphorothioate</td><td>≥ 960 g/kg<br>The following impurity is of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following level in the technical material:<br>Methanol max. 1 g/kg</td><td>1 September 2019</td><td>31 August 2034</td><td>Only for use on ornamentals and on potatoes.<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on tolclofos-methyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to aquatic organisms and mammals,<br>the risk to consumers, in particular the potential risk from metabolite DM-TM-CH<sub> 2 </sub>OH in potatoes,<br>the risk to operators, workers and bystanders;<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>139</td><td>Florpyrauxifen-benzyl<br>CAS No: 1390661-72-9<br>CIPAC No: 990.227</td><td>benzyl 4-amino-3-chloro-6-(4-chloro-2-fluoro-3-methoxyphenyl)-5-fluoropyridine-2-carboxylate</td><td>≥ 920 g/kg<br>The impurity toluene shall not exceed 3 g/kg in the technical material.</td><td>24 July 2019</td><td>24 July 2029</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on 22 March 2019, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of aquatic and terrestrial non-target plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures such as buffer zones and/or drift reduction nozzles, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority an updated assessment of the information submitted and, where relevant, further information to confirm the absence of endocrine activity in accordance with points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II to Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Commission Regulation (EU) 2018/605 by 24 July 2021.</td></tr>
<tr><td>140</td><td>Metalaxyl-M</td><td>methyl N-(methoxyacetyl)-N-(2,6-xylyl)-D-alaninate</td><td>≥ 920 g/kg</td><td>1 June 2020</td><td>31 May 2035</td><td>When used for seed treatment, only the treatment of seeds intended to be sown in greenhouses may be authorised.</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>CAS No 70630-17-0 (R)<br>CIPAC No 580</td><td></td><td>The following impurities are of toxicological concern and must not exceed the following levels in the technical material:<br>2,6-dimethylphenylamine:<br>max. content 0,5 g/kg<br>4-methoxy-5-methyl-5H-[1,2]oxathiole 2,2-dioxide:<br>max. content 1 g/kg<br>2-[(2,6-dimethyl-phenyl)-(2-methoxyacetyl)-amino]-propionic acid 1-methoxycarbonyl-ethyl ester:<br>max. content 0,18 g/kg</td><td></td><td></td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on metalaxyl-M, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured;<br>the protection of operators and workers, ensuring that the conditions of use prescribe the use of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>the protection of non-target arthropods, birds and mammals.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority an updated assessment of the information submitted and, where relevant, further information to confirm the absence of endocrine activity in accordance with points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II to Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Regulation (EU) 2018/605 by 26 May 2022.</td></tr>
<tr><td>141</td><td>Foramsulfuron<br>CAS No 173159-57-4<br>CIPAC No 659</td><td>1-(4,6-dimethoxypyrimidin-2-yl)-3-[2-(dimethylcarbamoyl)-5-formamidophenylsulfonyl]urea</td><td>≥ 973 g/kg</td><td>1 June 2020</td><td>31 May 2035</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on foramsulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk to consumers and operators,<br>the risk to aquatic organisms and non-target plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface water and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water, within two years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>142</td><td>Pyriproxyfen.<br>2-((1-(4-Phenoxyphenoxy)propan-2-yl)oxy)pyridine.<br>CIPAC No: 715.<br>CAS No: 95737-68-1.<br>EC No (Einecs or ELINCS): 429-800-1</td><td>4-phenoxyphenyl ( <em>RS)</em> -2(2-pyridyloxy) propyl ether</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg Max. impurity: Toluene<br>5 g/Kg</td><td>1 August 2020</td><td>31 July 2035</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on pyriproxyfen and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the dietary exposure of consumers to residues of pyriproxyfen,<br>the protection of sediment-dwelling organisms and aquatic organisms,<br>the protection of bees.<br>As regards the protection of sediment-dwelling organisms and aquatic organisms, for outdoor use of the plant protection products containing pyriproxyfen Member States shall include in the specific conditions appropriate risk mitigation measures, e.g. no-spray buffer zones and/or spray drift reduction, to achieve a low risk for sediment-dwelling organisms and aquatic organisms.<br>As regards the protection of bees, for outdoor use of plant protection products containing pyriproxyfen Member States shall include in the specific conditions a restriction of application to periods outside of the flowering of bee attractive crops, and appropriate risk mitigation measures, e.g. no-spray buffer zones and/or spray drift reduction, to achieve a low risk for bees and bee larvae.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the requested confirmatory information within two years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

### PART C — Basic Substances[^c000349]

General provisions applying to all substances listed in this Part: the Commission shall keep available all review reports (except for confidential information within the meaning of Article 63 of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009) for consultation by any interested parties or shall make them available to them on specific request.

<tr><td>21</td><td>L-cysteine (E 920)<br>CAS No: 52-89-1<br>EINECS: 200-157-7<br>(L-cysteine hydrochloride)<br>CAS No: 7048-04-6<br>EINECS: 615-117-8<br>(L-cysteine hydrochloride monohydrate)</td><td>L-cysteine hydrochloride (1:1)</td><td>Min. 98,0 % L-cysteine hydrochloride (on anhydrous basis)<br>Food grade in conformity with Commission Regulation (EU) No 231/2012.<br>max. 1,5 mg/kg As<br>max. 5 mg/kg Pb</td><td>2.6.2020</td><td>L-cysteine (E 920) shall be used as a mixture with matrix (flour, food grade) at a concentration of maximum 8% (of L-cysteine hydrochloride, on anhydrous basis) in accordance with the specific conditions included in the conclusions of the review report on L-cysteine (SANTE/11056/2019) and in particular Appendices I and II thereto.</td></tr>
<tr><td>22</td><td>Cow Milk<br>CAS No: 8049-98-7</td><td>Not available.</td><td>Not applicable</td><td>30.7.2020</td><td>Cow milk shall comply with Regulation (EC) No 1069/2009 and Commission Regulation (EU) No 142/2011.<br>Cow milk shall be used in accordance with the specific conditions included in the conclusions of the review report on cow milk (SANTE/12816/2019), and in particular Appendices I and II thereto.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

### PART D — Low-risk active substances'[^c000372]

General provisions applying to all substances listed in this Part: the Commission shall keep available all review reports (except for confidential information within the meaning of Article 63 of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009) for consultation by any interested parties or shall make them available to them on specific request.

### PART E — Candidates for substitution[^c000395]

<table>
<tfoot>
<tr><td colspan="7">Further details on identity and specification of active substance are provided in the review report.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">Commission Regulation (EU) 2018/605 of 19 April 2018 amending Annex II to Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009 by setting out scientific criteria for the determination of endocrine disrupting properties. (<a href="https://webarchive.nationalarchives.gov.uk/eu-exit/https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=uriserv:OJ.L_.2018.101.01.0033.01.ENG">OJ L 101, 20.4.2018, p. 33</a>).</td></tr>
</tfoot>
<tbody>
<tr><th></th><th>Common Name, Identification Numbers</th><th>IUPAC Name</th><th>Purity</th><th>Date of approval</th><th>Expiration of approval</th><th>Specific provisions</th></tr>
<tr><td>1</td><td>Flumetralin<br>CAS No 62924-70-3<br>CIPAC No 971</td><td><em>N</em> -(2-chloro-6-fluorobenzyl)- <em>N</em> -ethyl-α,α,α-trifluoro-2,6-dinitro- <em>p</em> -toluidine</td><td>980 g/kg<br>The impurity Nitrosamine (calculated as nitroso-dimethylamine) shall not exceed 0,001 g/kg in the technical material.</td><td>11 December 2015</td><td>11 December 2022</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on flumetralin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>((a)) the protection of operators and workers, ensuring that conditions of use include the application of adequate personal protective equipment, where appropriate;<br>((b)) the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>((c)) the risk to herbivorous mammals;<br>((d)) the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production);<br>2.. the compliance of the toxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information referred to in points 1 and 2 by 11 June 2016.</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td>Esfenvalerate<br>CAS No: 66230-04-4<br>CIPAC No: 481</td><td>(αS)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (2S)-2-(4-chlorophenyl)-3-methylbutyrate</td><td>830 g/kg<br>The impurity toluene shall not exceed 10 g/kg in the technical material.</td><td>1 January 2016</td><td>31 December 2022</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on esfenvalerate, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the risk from esfenvalerate and the 2SαR-isomer of fenvalerate to aquatic organisms including the risk for bio-accumulation through the food chain,<br>the risk to honeybees and non-target arthropods,<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>3</td><td>Metsulfuron-methyl<br>CAS No 74223-64-6<br>CIPAC No 441.201</td><td>Methyl 2-(4-methoxy-6-methyl-1,3,5-triazin-2-ylcarbamoylsulfamoyl)benzoate</td><td>967 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2016</td><td>31 March 2023</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on metsulfuron-methyl, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of consumers,<br>the protection of groundwater,<br>the protection of non-target terrestrial plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 30 September 2016 confirmatory information as regards the genotoxic potential of the metabolite triazine-amine (IN-A4098) to confirm that this metabolite is not genotoxic and not relevant for risk assessment.</td></tr>
<tr><td>4</td><td>Benzovindiflupyr<br>CAS No: 1072957-71-1<br>CIPAC No: not available</td><td><em>N</em> -[(1 <em>RS</em> ,4 <em>SR</em> )-9-(dichloromethylene)-1,2,3,4-tetrahydro-1,4-methanonaphthalen-5-yl]-3-(difluoromethyl)-1-methylpyrazole-4-carboxamide</td><td>960 g/kg (50/50) racemate</td><td>2.3.2016</td><td>2.3.2023</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on benzovindiflupyr, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the risk to aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>((1)) the technical specification of the active substance as manufactured (based on commercial scale production) including the relevance of impurities;<br>((2)) the compliance of the toxicity and ecotoxicity batches with the confirmed technical specification;<br>((3)) the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface water and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority the information requested under points (1) and (2) by 2 September 2016 and the information requested under point (3) within two years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>5</td><td>Lambda-Cyhalothrin<br>CAS No 91465-08-6<br>CIPAC No 463</td><td>A 1:1 mixture of:<br>(R)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1S,3S)-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate and (S)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1R,3R)-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate or of (R)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1S)-cis-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate and (S)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1R)-cis-3-[(Z)-2-chloro-3,3,3-trifluoropropenyl]-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate</td><td>900 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2016</td><td>31 March 2023</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on lambda-cyhalothrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to the:<br>((a)) protection of operators, workers and bystanders;<br>((b)) metabolites potentially formed in processed commodities;<br>((c)) risk to aquatic organisms, mammals and non-target arthropods.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicants shall submit confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. a systematic review to assess the evidence available as regards potential sperm effects linked to exposure to lambda-cyhalothrin using guidance available (e.g. EFSA GD on Systematic Review methodology, 2010);<br>2.. toxicological information to assess the toxicological profile of the metabolites V (PBA) and XXIII (PBA(OH)).<br>The applicants shall submit those information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 1 April 2018.</td></tr>
<tr><td>6</td><td>Prosulfuron<br>CAS No 94125-34-5<br>CIPAC No 579</td><td>1-(4-methoxy-6-methyl-triazin-2-yl)-3-[2-(3,3,3- trifluoropropyl)-phenylsulfonyl]-urea</td><td>950 g/kg<br>The impurity 2-(3,3,3-trifluoro-propyl)-benzene sulphonamide shall not exceed 10 g/kg in the technical material.</td><td>1 May 2017</td><td>30 April 2024</td><td>PART A<br>Use shall be limited to one application every three years on the same field at a maximum dose of 20 g active substance per hectare.<br>PART B<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on prosulfuron, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions;<br>the risk to non-target terrestrial and aquatic plants.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information as regards the genotoxic potential of the metabolite triazine-amine (CGA150829) to confirm that this metabolite is not genotoxic and not relevant for risk assessment.<br>The applicant shall submit that information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority by 31 October 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td>7</td><td>Pendimethalin<br>CAS No 40487-42-1<br>CIPAC No 357</td><td>N-(1-ethylpropyl)-2,6-dinitro-3,4-xylidene</td><td>900 g/kg<br>1,2-dichloroethane<br>≤ 1 g/kg<br>Total N-Nitroso compounds: max 100 ppm, of which N-Nitroso-pendimethalin: &lt; 45 ppm.</td><td>1 September 2017</td><td>31 August 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on pendimethalin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the specification of the technical material as commercially manufactured, which must be confirmed and supported by appropriate analytical data. The test material used in the toxicity dossiers shall be compared and verified against the specification of the technical material,<br>the protection of operators,<br>the protection of birds, mammals and aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>In particular, personal protective equipment such as gloves, coverall and sturdy footwear has to be worn to ensure that the AOEL is not exceeded for the operator.<br>The applicant shall submit confirmatory information to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority as regards:<br>1.. the potential for bioaccumulation, in particular a reliable BCF value for bluegill sunfish ( <em>Lepomis macrochirus</em> );<br>2.. the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater are abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the confirmatory information requested under point 1 by 31 December 2018. The applicant shall submit the confirmatory information requested under point 2 within a period of two years of the publication by the Commission of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.</td></tr>
<tr><td>8</td><td>Imazamox<br>CAS No 114311-32-9<br>CIPAC No 619</td><td>2-[(RS)-4-isopropyl-4-methyl-5-oxo-2-imidazolin-2-yl]-5-methoxymethylnicotinic acid</td><td>≥ 950 g/kg<br>The impurity cyanide ion (CN<sup> – </sup>) shall not exceed 5 mg/kg in the technical material.</td><td>1 November 2017</td><td>31 October 2024</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on imazamox, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of consumers;<br>the protection of aquatic plants and of non-target terrestrial plants;<br>the protection of groundwater, when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climatic conditions.<br>Conditions of authorisation shall include risk mitigation measures and monitoring programs shall be initiated to verify potential groundwater contamination from imazamox and metabolites CL 312622 and CL 354825 in vulnerable zones, where appropriate.</td></tr>
<tr><td>9</td><td>Propyzamide<br>CAS No 23950-58-5<br>CIPAC No 315</td><td>3,5-dichloro- <em>N</em> -(1,1-dimethylprop-2-ynyl) benzamide</td><td>920 g/kg</td><td>1 July 2018</td><td>30 June 2025</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on propyzamide and in particular Appendices I and II thereof, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators,<br>the protection of groundwater in vulnerable areas,<br>the protection of birds, mammals, non-target plants, soil and aquatic organisms.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>In particular, personal protective equipment such as gloves, coverall and sturdy footwear has to be worn to ensure that the AOEL is not exceeded for the operator.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. the completion of assessment of toxicological profile of metabolites identified in significant concentration in primary and rotational crops;<br>2.. the soil degradation of major metabolite RH- 24580;<br>3.. the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater are abstracted for drinking water.<br>The applicant shall submit the information mentioned under point (1) by 31 October 2018 and the information mentioned under point (2) by 30 April 2019. The applicant shall submit the confirmatory information mentioned in point (3) within two years after a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater be made public by the Commission.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="6">10</td><td>Copper compounds:</td><td></td><td></td><td rowspan="6">1 January 2019</td><td rowspan="6">31 December 2025</td><td rowspan="6">Only uses resulting in a total application of maximum 28 kg of copper per hectare over a period of 7 years shall be authorised.<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009 of the European Parliament and of the Council, the conclusions of the review report on copper compounds and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the operator, worker and bystander safety and ensure that conditions of use prescribe the application of adequate personal protective equipment and other mitigation measures as appropriate;<br>the protection of water and non-target organisms. In relation to these identified risks, risk mitigation measures, such as buffer zones, shall be applied where appropriate;<br>the amount of active substance applied and ensure that the authorised amounts, in terms of rates and number of applications, do not exceed the minimum necessary to achieve the desired effects and do not cause any unacceptable effect on the environment taking into account background levels of copper at the application site, and, where the information is available, copper input from other sources. Member States may in particular decide to set a maximum annual application rate not exceeding 4 kg/ha of copper.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Copper hydroxide CAS No 20427-59-2 CIPAC No 44.305</td><td>Copper (II) hydroxide</td><td>≥ 573 g/kg</td></tr>
<tr><td>Copper oxychloride CAS No 1332-65-6 or 1332-40-7 CIPAC No 44.602</td><td>Dicopper chloride trihydroxide</td><td>≥ 550 g/kg</td></tr>
<tr><td>Copper oxide CAS No 1317-39-1 CIPAC No 44.603</td><td>Copper oxide</td><td>≥ 820 g/kg</td></tr>
<tr><td>Bordeaux mixture CAS No 8011-63-0 CIPAC No 44.604</td><td>Not allocated</td><td>≥ 245 g/kg</td></tr>
<tr><td>Tribasic copper sulphate CAS No 12527-76-3 CIPAC No 44.306</td><td>Not allocated</td><td>≥ 490 g/kg<br>The following impurities shall not exceed the following levels:<br>Arsenic max. 0,1 mg/g Cu<br>Cadmium max. 0,1 mg/g Cu<br>Lead max. 0,3 mg/g Cu<br>Nickel max. 1 mg/g Cu<br>Cobalt max. 3 mg/kg<br>Mercury max. 5 mg/kg<br>Chromium max. 100 mg/kg<br>Antimony max. 7 mg/kg</td></tr>
<tr><td>11</td><td>Methoxyfenozide<br>CAS No 161050-58-4<br>CIPAC No 656</td><td>N-tert-Butyl-N′-(3-methoxy-o-toluoyl)-3,5-xylohydrazide</td><td>≥ 970 g/kg<br>The following impurities must not exceed the following levels in the technical material:<br>Tert-butylhydrazine &lt; 0,001 g/kg<br>RH-116267 &lt; 2 g/kg</td><td>1 April 2019</td><td>31 March 2026</td><td>Only uses in greenhouses shall be authorised.<br>For the implementation of the uniform principles as referred to in Article 29(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the review report on methoxyfenozide, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.<br>In their overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of groundwater when the substance is applied in regions with vulnerable soil and/or climate conditions;<br>the risk of accumulation in soil;<br>the protection of non-target arthropods, sediment dwelling and aquatic organisms;<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. a comparative in vitro metabolism study on methoxyfenozide, by 1 April 2020;<br>2.. the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water, within 2 years after adoption of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.<br>The applicant shall also provide an updated assessment of the information submitted and, where relevant, further information to confirm the absence of thyroid endocrine activity in accordance with Points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Commission Regulation (EU) 2018/605 by 1 February 2021.</td></tr>
<tr><td>12</td><td>alpha-cypermethrin<br>CAS No 67375-30-8<br>CIPAC No 454</td><td>Racemate comprising:<br>(R)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1S,3S)-3-(2,2-dichlorovinyl)-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate and (S)-α-cyano-3-phenoxybenzyl (1R,3R)-3-(2,2-dichlorovinyl)-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate<br>or<br>(R)-α-cyano-3 phenoxybenzyl-(1S)-cis-3-(2,2-dichlorovinyl)-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate<br>and (S)-α-cyano-3 phenoxybenzyl-(1R)-cis-3-(2,2-dichlorovinyl)-2,2-dimethylcyclopropanecarboxylate</td><td>≥ 980 g/kg<br>The manufacturing impurity hexane is considered to be of toxicological concern and must not exceed 1 g/kg in the technical material</td><td>1 November 2019</td><td>31 October 2026</td><td>For the implementation of the uniform principles, as referred to in Article 9(6) of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, the conclusions of the renewal report on alpha-cypermethrin, and in particular Appendices I and II thereto, shall be taken into account.In this overall assessment Member States shall pay particular attention to:<br>the protection of operators, ensuring that the conditions of use prescribe the application of adequate personal protective equipment;<br>the consumer risk assessment;<br>the protection of aquatic organisms, bees and non-target arthropods.<br>Conditions of use shall include risk mitigation measures, where appropriate.<br>The applicant shall submit to the Commission, the Member States and the Authority confirmatory information as regards:<br>1.. the toxicological profile of the metabolites bearing the 3-phenoxybenzoyl moiety;<br>2.. the potential relative toxicity of individual cypermethrin isomers, in particular the enantiomer (1S cis αR);<br>3.. the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater, when surface water or groundwater is abstracted for drinking water;<br>4.. Points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Regulation (EU) 2018/605.<br>The applicant shall submit the information referred to in point 1 by 30 October 2020; the information referred to in point 2 within two years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on evaluation of isomer mixtures; and the information referred to in point 3 within two years from the date of publication, by the Commission, of a guidance document on evaluation of the effect of water treatment processes on the nature of residues present in surface and groundwater.<br>As regards Points 3.6.5 and 3.8.2 of Annex II of Regulation (EC) No 1107/2009, as amended by Regulation (EU) 2018/605 an updated assessment of the information already submitted and, where relevant, further information to confirm the absence of androgenic endocrine activity shall be submitted by 30 October 2021.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
